# E16-D06 — Accessibility review and engineering hand-off for the recorder design set (SCR-140, SCR-141, SCR-142, SCR-047, SCR-151, CMP-177)

**Ticket:** E16-D06 (#374) · **Epic:** E16 Recorder · **Kind:** WCAG 2.2 AA design review (Part A, desk only) and design hand-off pack (Part B), text only, one PR which ends the ticket (RULED BQ-01, BQ-18). The accessibility-specialist sign-off is the orchestrator's design approval under the 2026-10-29 delegation plus the QA record (RULED BQ-21); "Security engineer reviewed" is the light security lens record posted on #374 over the frozen set of §20.3 (RULED BQ-19); the owner walkthrough is "owner walkthrough: post-Done (#2701)" (§24.3). Nothing here claims the owner's `approved`.

**Ruling status: RULED.** The twenty-four questions of the BQ list (#374 issuecomment-6516299835, read at base `e87be9b978`) were ruled by the orchestrator on 2026-10-30 (**R374**, #374 issuecomment-6516426184, under the 2026-10-29 delegation): all twenty-four defaults as proposed, with precisions; the eight asked by name — BQ-04, BQ-06, BQ-09, BQ-17, BQ-18, BQ-19, BQ-20 (pointer lines, no ticks) and BQ-23 — confirmed. Every "RULED BQ-nn" stamp below points at §R ("Rulings applied"), which records each default as ruled. Where this file says PROPOSED it means the review's own default, approved at the orchestrator's design approval of the PR and lifted into the #2701 ledger from §1.6 (R374 BQ-07).

**Scope** (T Scope, four bullets): Part A — the keyboard operability of every control (§3), the focus model (§4), the region map (§5), the exact announcement strings for recording started / stopped, a disk warning crossed, a purge in progress / finished and a gap under the data cursor (§6), the announcement budget (§7), G-T2 on every gated control (§8), the R1–R7 matrix (§9), the WCAG 2.2 cross-walk with contrast in three themes, CVD, motion, targets and tabular figures (§10), the findings register (§11) and the rows (§12), the `05` §10 script per surface (§13) and the keyboard-only five-task scenario (§14). Part B — the hand-off package: status and evidence (§15), the data contract with the API fields each element binds to (§16), the redlines and the token map (§17), the state matrix (§18), the component inventory (§19), the copy deck and the a11y script as one QA artefact with the per-screen checklist and the frozen security-bearing set (§20), the asset routing (§21), the written engineering walkthrough (§22), the design-ahead record and the waivers (§23), the hand-offs, the DoD, the sign-off, the `14` edit set and the deviations (§24).

**Start.** `blocked_by` E16-D03 (#324, closed 2026-10-28), E16-D04 (#372, closed 2026-10-29) and E16-D05 (#373, closed 2026-10-29), all merged and Done; contributing and Done: E16-D02 (#323, closed 2026-10-24) with its CMP-177 hand-off checklist (`D02H`), E16-D01 (#322). Review and hand-off precedents: E15-D15 (#369) and E13-D06 (#347), the two forms this file joins. **Named only, never pinned or quoted:** E16-D07 (#565), E16-S01 (#484), E16-S02 (#566), E16-S03 (#485), E16-S04 (#486), E16-S05 (#487), E16-Q01 (#404), E16-Q02 (#479), E16-Q03 (#480), E16-Q05 (#482), E16-T09 (#567). The closed first implementers the parents name for their contract gaps — E16-T04 (#488), E16-T06 (#490), E16-T07 (#491), E16-T08 (#492), E16-T10 (#493) — are Done with the gaps unserved (§16.3; RULED BQ-22).

**Inputs pinned.** Every `KEY`:line cite below is read at `origin/main` by the tool and pinned to the words on that line in `docs/design/_tools/e16_d06_ruled_pins.txt` (the tool's G13-CITE; `--repin-ruled` is the pins' only writer). The BQ list read every cite at `e87be9b978`; between it and the review's base only `15` (one line), `16` (a 3-line shift) and `23` (one line) changed, so the `16` cites are re-pointed in §1.5 and every other cite stands as listed. The `14` cites are read with this PR's own five pointer lines removed, so they hold before and after the merge.

**Key.** `NN`:line = `docs/plan/NN-*.md` (`22` = `22-api-openapi.yaml`). `D02`…`D05` = `docs/design/E16/E16-D0n.md`; `D02H` = `docs/design/E16/E16-D02-handoff.md`; `D01` = `docs/design/research/e16-recorder-mental-model.md`; `E09-D09`, `E42-D02`, `E11-D04`, `E12-D02` = `docs/design/<epic>/<ticket>.md`; `DS` = `.claude/rules/32-design-specs.md`; `README` = `docs/design/README.md`; `SEED` = `services/api/candleviewer/auth/rbac_seed.json`; `CAL` = `docs/plan/backlog/_tools/calendar_cv.py`; `ICONS` = `packages/ui/src/icons/registry.ts`. `T` = the ticket text (#374; its JSON twin `docs/plan/backlog/E16.json`, key `E16-D06`): `T Context`, `T Scope` (four bullets), `T OOS`, `T AC1`–`AC3`, `T Tech`, `T Test`, `T Sec`, `T a11y`, `T Perf`, `T DoD` (four lines), `T Branch`, `T Brief`, `T Adapt`. Rulings of record, cited and never re-asked: **R374** (this ticket's, above), **R369** (E15-D15), **R347** (E13-D06), **R316** (E13-D05), **R918** (E30-D05, its ledger row **A30**), **L1-n** (#2701 design-defaults ledger 1 row n; row 119 the sign-off form), **DIR** (the owner directive of 2026-10-29), **A28** (the post-Done owner-walkthrough form); the parents' own: D02's decisions 1–5 (`D02`:24–:30) and the lever-4 cap (`D02`:794), D03 BQ-1…20 (`D03`:36–:61), D04 BQ-1…22 with ledger 1 rows 57–59 (`D04`:25–:52, :117–:141), D05 BQ-1…20 (`D05`:25–:50). Ids: **KB** keyboard row, **FM** focus row, **RG** region row, **SR** script row, **BG** budget rule, **T2** gated row, **SD** safety-disclosure row, **CR** carrier row, **KS** walkthrough step, **F** finding, **C** contradiction, **G** gap, **Q** open question, **A** copy-ready amendment, **HR** heuristic, **D** deviation.

**Numbers.** Every count and measured value here (rows per table, ratios, greyscale ΔL\*, CVD ΔE76, the image counts, the deck counts, the sprint dates) is computed by `docs/design/_tools/e16_d06_a11y_handoff_audit.py`, written by its `--fill` and re-derived under `--check`; none is typed from memory. Design parameters quoted from the parents (10 s, 2 s, 60 s, 30 min, 32 px, 24 px, 48 px, 960 px, 85 vh, 366 days) are theirs and are cited.

## 0. Status, evidence and the boards (no new boards)

**No new boards and no new Penpot page** (RULED BQ-17). The evidence is the 52 <!-- audit:images=52 --> merged E16 PNGs under `docs/design/E16/img/`, by parent D02 / D03 / D04 / D05: 14/14/10/14 <!-- audit:images-by-parent=14/14/10/14 -->, of which 12 <!-- audit:cvd-images=12 --> are the committed greyscale and deuteranopia renders (D02 boards 01 and 06, `D02`:809–:812; D03 boards 03 and 06, `D03`:93–:96; D05 boards 02 and 05, `D05`:69; none for D04 — F-15). The annotated a11y layer is this file's tables plus the boards a reviewer needs: D02 board 17 `stop-refused` (`D02`:803), board 20 `CMP-177/states` (:805), board 21 `REC-chip/states` (:806) and board 23 `redlines` (:807); D03 board 08 `keyboard-a11y-copy` (`D03`:89); D04 board 08 (`D04`:80); D05 board 08 (`D05`:78). The three siblings' frame registers hold 41/41/41 <!-- audit:frames=41/41/41 --> frames (D03 / D04 / D05, each gated by its own tool); D02's ten drawn boards and thirteen text-bound states are §18's first rows.

**Nothing of E16 is built.** `packages/ui/src/components/EmptyState.tsx` (CMP-026, with its stories) is the only shipped piece any surface here composes; `apps/web` holds no recorder, retention or storage surface and the keymap holds no recorder key, so every evidence cell that would name a built behaviour reads "not yet built — E16-S0n", and the real assistive-technology pass is E16-Q05's (#482, Sprint 08).

## 1. Purpose, method, locked inputs and the rules stated once

### 1.1 What this review fixes

The accessibility contract of the recorder design set as the consuming stories build it: one keyboard model (§3), one focus destination per operation (§4), the region map (§5), the announcement script and its budget (§6, §7), the T2 table over every gated control (§8), the R1–R7 matrix (§9), the clause verdicts, the WCAG 2.2 cross-walk and the colour audits (§10), the findings register (§11), the rows (§12), the script E16-Q05 verifies against the build (§13) and the written walkthrough (§14) — and, as the hand-off, what the stories bind to (§16), what they draw (§17–§19), what QA runs (§20) and who takes what (§24). It edits no merged text: a defect in a merged design is an F row, a plan-text change is an A row (§12.4), a disagreement between merged texts or with the ticket is a C row (§12.1). `git diff` on `D02`…`D05`, `D02H` and `15` is empty (RULED BQ-05; the tool's G22-EDIT-SET).

### 1.2 Method (honest)

A desk review, clause by clause, of E16-D02 through E16-D05 as merged against `05` §3–§7 and §10, with `14` SCR-140 / 141 / 142 / 047 / 151, `15`'s contracts, E09-D09's T2 grammar, E42-D02 §9.1's rung model, E11-D04 §11.3's exits and `DS` G-T2 and R1–R7 as the binding neighbours (the E13-D05, E29-D05, E30-D05 and E15-D15 method). Every verdict carries `KEY`:line evidence and, where a merged board draws the case, a board number. The tool imports, never copies: `contrast_check.py`'s ratio, `docs/research/_tools/cvd_simulate.py`'s projection and ΔE76, the E16-D05 audit's token resolver and the E16-D03 / D04 / D05 audits' pair lists, plus E16-D02 §10.4's rows parsed at the base. No screen reader was run and no participant was seen; every evaluative sentence that rests on judgement is marked `(heuristic)`; the assistive technologies appear only in the routing sentence of §14 (R316 BQ-8).

### 1.4 Rules stated once (every later row cites "R-n")

- **R-1 Severity (RULED BQ-04).** **Blocking:** a `05` clause or a WCAG 2.2 A / AA criterion with no compliant path in the merged designs; a state carried by colour alone; an announcement with no string or politeness; a G-T2 miss on a gated control (`DS`:15 makes it Major in a spec; here the control is the object of review, as R918 BQ-04 widened it); an operation whose focus destination is unstated or lands on a removed element, **read precisely**: no merged text _and no merged rule_ yields it. Where `05`:96 yields it because the trigger survives, or where E09-D09 §9.4's rule (`E09-D09`:250) in its form, applied by analogy (RULED BQ-04 (b)) — the line literally covers a route replacement or a `revoked` unmount: after the unmount, the nearest stable heading or placeholder, after the announcement — yields it because every parent pins that rule as a locked input (`D02`:19; `D03`:11; `D04`:11; `D05`:11), the row is a **Minor** that makes the destination explicit (F-03…F-06). **Major:** an at-risk clause, a T2 over- or under-conversion. **Minor:** wording, cadence, order, a destination a rule yields but no text states. **Info:** recorded for a consumer's charter. `05`:238's P0 bar is E16-Q05's and is not used for design findings.
- **R-2 Findings and their closing (RULED BQ-05).** An F row names the merged sentence, the replacement, one owner (the spec and its area: `E16-D0n · area/recorder-replay`, never a person or a lane) and one target: the consuming story that builds the finding's version — E16-S01 (#484) for SCR-140 and CMP-177, E16-S03 (#485) for SCR-141, E16-S04 (#486) for SCR-142, E16-S05 (#487) for SCR-047 / SCR-151, E16-S02 (#566) for the REC chip — plus "the parent at its next text touch" (R347's HO-07 form); closed parents are never reopened and nothing is filed. Every F row carries a **`G-T2`** cell (the parents' class words: `T2 · 2a` / `2b` / `sentence` · `validity` · `busy` · `status` · `absence` · `outcome` · `T4` · `n/a`) and a **`Focus`** cell (`kept · <element>` · `moves to · <element>` · `returns to · <trigger>` · `restored to · <heading>` · `n/a`); the tool fails a row with either empty. **The hold (`T a11y`; `02`:53):** a Blocking F row stamps E16-S01 (#484), E16-S03 (#485) and E16-S04 (#486) "cannot enter Ready until fixed or an exception is recorded on #374" and holds nothing on the parents' standing approvals.
- **R-3 Verdicts.** `compliant` · `at risk` · `non-compliant` · `out of scope — <owner>` · `not yet decidable — routed to E16-Q05 (#482)`. A `non-compliant` clause carries a Blocking F row; an `at risk` clause carries a Major F row or names its interim path in the verdict cell — `at risk — interim path: <the consuming story's build rule>` (E29-D05 R-3's form). A clause or criterion any Minor-or-above F row cites is never `compliant` and its Row cell names those rows (G9-VERDICT).
- **R-4 Focus follows the table (RULED BQ-07; `T AC1`).** Every operation has one destination in §4, named as an element that exists after the operation; the parents' rules are lifted, never re-decided; a destination no merged text states carries its F row with a PROPOSED default where `05`:96 or `E09-D09`:250 supplies its form. Focus is never moved by a 1 Hz update, a poll, a job tick or a background event (`05`:96; `D02`:528; `D03`:350).
- **R-5 One voice.** A dialog, a drawer or a popover is read by role as focus enters it (never a second live utterance, R369 BQ-11); a T2 press re-shows its reason once, politely, through the shell announcer (`E09-D09`:243; `D02`:646), never the summary region; a live region is pre-rendered empty and only its text changes (`05`:150).
- **R-6 No data in a reason, no scope in a string.** A `recorder.*` or `rbac.*` reason names a capability, never a symbol, count, size or user name (except the view-as target) (`E09-D09`:85; `D02`:510); a `role_restricted` symbol reads "Recording status restricted", never "Not recording" (`D02`:340); example strings keep the parents' fixture words (`BTCUSDT`, `ETHUSDT`, `SOLUSDT`, `ADAUSDT`, `alex`) and never an account or a balance.

- `16` gained three lines above its §11: §11.4 :511 → **:514**, §12 :620 → **:623**, §13.3 :674 → **:677**. `15` and `23` changed one line each outside the cited set, so CMP-177 (`15`:2188), CMP-212 (`15`:2679) and the `recorder` topic (`23`:865, :893, :4084) stand.
- The BQ list's icon mapping (`lock` → `Locked`, `pin` → `Pins`, …) was a guess at the registry's names; at the base the registry keys are the parents' own words (`lock`, `pin`, `info`, `warning`, `danger`, `success`, `error`, `disconnected`, `calendar`, `minus`), and no `clock` glyph exists (§21.1; C-q).

## 4. The focus model (AC1; R-4; RULED BQ-07)

One row per operation, lifted from the parents. **Destination after** names an element that exists after the operation; the last column is the "never a removed element" proof. A destination no merged text states carries a PROPOSED default and its F row; the tool fails a row whose destination is empty, reads "body" or leaves the vocabulary (G4-FOCUS). 18 <!-- audit:fm=18 --> rows.

**What the table settles (RULED BQ-07).** (a) The typed dialogs' first focus is Cancel (C-a; F-01, F-02). (b) **Keep recording pressed:** no text names the destination; the Why? button survives, so `05`:96 yields "returns to Why?" as `Esc` does — F-03, Minor, the PROPOSED default FM-05. (c) **Undo pressed:** the staged row returns to normal and Undo gives way to Remove in place — `D02`:561's "what replaced it in place" applied; F-04, Minor, PROPOSED "focus stays on the cell, now Remove". (d) **The tier card's Retry** after a successful re-poll: the Retry goes; E09-D09 §9.4's rule (`E09-D09`:250) in its form, applied by analogy (RULED BQ-04 (b)), yields the card's heading — F-05, Minor under R-1 (b). (e) **SCR-047's exit while the block held focus:** `E11-D04`:352 names the exit, not the focus; the CTA and the link unmount; `05`:96 forbids a move on a data update and E09-D09 §9.4's rule (`E09-D09`:250), applied by analogy (RULED BQ-04 (b)), names the restoration — F-06, Minor, PROPOSED "restored to the chart region's toolbar, its nearest stable stop in `E12-D02`:338's order, never to the CTA's route"; no string invented. (f) `D02`:528's rule and `D03`:350's two user-caused moves are compliant, cited. The run is E16-Q02's (#479) and E16-Q05's (#482).

## 6. The screen-reader announcement script (AC2; RULED BQ-08)

### 6.1 The script table

One row per event. **Strings are verbatim from the merged decks**, with their cite; a PROPOSED string is a parent's own PROPOSED row or this review's §1.6 default, marked; a G row names where no merged string exists and proposes none. **Politeness** is `polite`, `assertive`, `none` (read by role as focus enters, no live region) or `never`. **Host** is the region of §5. The tool checks every quoted string against the cited parent and mirrors the table into the deck's `script` (G6-SCRIPT). 35 <!-- audit:sr=35 --> rows.

### 6.2 The politeness resolutions (RULED BQ-09)

- **(a) Job progress.** `D03`:343 announces the sweep's progress "≤ once per 5 s" on the 2 s poll; `D04`:357 announces a job's start and its terminal state and "never per tick, never per 2 s poll"; `D02`:543 batches state changes ≤ once per 10 s. D04's rule governs every job strip and footer (BG-02) — C-b, **F-09** (Minor) to D03 §10 via E16-S03; progress stays visual text with `aria-valuetext` (`D03`:296).
- **(b) The error summary.** `D03`:289 and :340 give it `role="alert"` and :350 moves focus to it — the double-speak R918 BQ-12 ruled against (R369 BQ-12 (b)): **no `role="alert"` on the summary while focus moves to it** — C-c, **F-10** (Minor) to D03 §7 / §10 via E16-S03; `D04`:355's form (focus moves, no alert role) is the compliant one.
- **(c) The succeeded purge.** `D02`:204 names no politeness; `D04`:357's "one per terminal state", polite, covers SCR-142 only — C-m, **F-08** (Minor) to D02 §3.2 via E16-S01 (SR-12).
- Everything else keeps its merged level. **The assertive set, closed:** the level-3 banner and the incident banner (SR-07, SR-08; `D02`:546), purge failed (SR-10; `D02`:200), retention run failed (SR-23; `D03`:345), import failed (SR-26; `D04`:357) — each `role="alert"` once, never repeating while the condition holds; everything else polite once; a T2 press polite once through the shell announcer (SR-14).

### 6.3 The four named strings (`T Scope` bullet 2)

1. **Recording started / stopped.** Stopped = the batch grammar "ETHUSDT degraded; SOLUSDT stopped recording" (SR-01, SR-02; `D02`:545). Started = **no string in SCR-140's region**: the batch grammar implies "{SYMBOL} started recording" but no merged text says it, and the chart's "Recording started 10:22 UTC" (`D05`:314) is E12-D02's — **G-01**, a G row to E16-D02 at its next text touch and a build rule for E16-S01; nothing is proposed (C-l).
2. **A disk warning crossed.** Level 1 and level 2 "speak in the disk card (warning icon + words, polite)" (`D02`:166) with the headline strings of `D02`:494–:495, but no live region is named for the crossing (the summary region carries "the summary line without its timestamp", `D02`:543) — **F-07** (Minor) to D02 §9.3 with the PROPOSED default of §1.6 item 4: the level headline enters the 10 s batch once per `level` change (SR-06). Level 3 = the alert once (SR-07; `D02`:167, :546), compliant.
3. **A purge in progress / finished.** In progress = the busy control (`aria-busy`, `D02`:420) and the `role="status"` purge line (`D02`:183) with **no start string** — **G-02** (E16-D02 / E16-S01), the line's cadence under BG-02 (SR-09). Finished: failed `role="alert"` once (SR-10), cancelled and unknown polite (SR-11), succeeded polite once under **F-08** (SR-12).
4. **A gap under the data cursor.** "after a recording gap 02:11–02:23 UTC" on the first slot out, and the tooltip's title and range inside (SR-33; `D05`:296, :317) — compliant, cited; the band itself is never announced (SR-32; `D05`:316), the chip at mount only (SR-34; `D05`:318).

## 7. The announcement budget (AC2; RULED BQ-09)

| Id    | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Source                                                |
| ----- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| BG-01 | **SCR-140's summary region** announces changes of state only, batched, **at most once every 10 s**; changes in the same window are one batch, so none is lost; ticking numbers (rates, sizes, lag, percentages) are never announced; the 1 Hz values change in place with tabular figures — `T AC2` verbatim, compliant                        | `D02`:543–:545; `D02`:524–:527; `D02H`:110–:112       |
| BG-02 | **Job progress — `D04`'s rule for every job strip and footer:** **one announcement at a job's start and one per terminal state**, never per tick or per poll; progress stays visual text with a CMP-023 whose `aria-valuetext` equals the text; `D03`:343's "≤ once per 5 s" is C-b (F-09); `05`:147's ambient cadence is for prices, not jobs | `D04`:357; `D03`:296; `D03`:343                       |
| BG-03 | **The error summary** takes focus and carries no `role="alert"` (R918 BQ-12; R369 BQ-12 (b)); server refusals at the point of action are polite once, inline, and never move focus except the summary's one user-caused move (F-10)                                                                                                            | `D04`:355; `D03`:350; `D04`:172                       |
| BG-04 | **Ambient values never:** a rate, a size, a lag, a percentage, a lingering countdown or the sample stamp is never announced per tick; the chip's numbers reach the accessible name at mount only; `05`:147's "1 update / 2 s per field" default is for price values and no E16 row claims it                                                   | `D02H`:110–:112; `D05`:318; `D04`:357; `05`:147       |
| BG-05 | **The assertive set, closed:** the level-3 banner, the incident banner, purge failed, retention run failed, import failed — each `role="alert"` once, never repeating while the condition holds; everything else polite once; a T2 press polite once through the shell announcer, never the summary region                                     | `D02`:546; `D02`:200; `D03`:345; `D04`:357; `D02`:646 |
| BG-06 | **Precedence:** trading and connection announcements (E30's arm / disarm, fills, E17's states) are `05`:148's class and pre-empt; recorder announcements are polite and coalesce; **coalescing never swallows an assertive row** (R369 BQ-13's precision): a level-3 crossing inside a 10 s batch is spoken as the alert after the batch       | `05`:148; `D02`:546; `D02`:543                        |
| BG-07 | **SCR-047 / SCR-151:** the block region speaks once per reason entry; the cursor's readout per move, never per tick; the first-bar outcome once; a gap band or seam is never announced; the chip at mount only                                                                                                                                 | `D05`:312–:318                                        |

7 <!-- audit:bg=7 --> rules. AC2's number for SCR-140 is **one polite summary per 10 s window**, plus any assertive row the window trips, spoken after the batch (BG-06).

## 8. Gated controls — the T2 table and the safety disclosures (RULED BQ-10)

### 8.1 The T2 table

**The rule as applied** (`DS`:17–:61; `E09-D09`:85; `E09-D09`:243; `D02`:641–:646): a T2 control keeps its native role and its full label (G-T2.1a); it is `aria-disabled="true"`, never `disabled`, so it stays in the Tab order and a menu item stays arrow-reachable (G-T2.1b); the reason node is in the DOM at mount and linked by `aria-describedby`, in one of three renderings — **2a** inline, **2b** the CMP-016 echo (the grid's Action cell only, D-15), the **sentence** form (no control exists) (G-T2.1c); CMP-079 wraps every role leg and the CMP-019 `lock` ("Not available") leads the label where a control exists (G-T2.1d); label `color.text.tertiary`, reason `color.text.secondary`, the fill `color.surface.raised` with a `color.border.default` hairline, never `color.text.disabled` or `opacity.disabled` (G-T2.2); `Enter`, `Space` or a bound key changes nothing and re-shows the reason once, politely (G-T2.3; SR-14); a reason is one of E09-D09 §3.1's keys or a registered state key, one sentence ≤ 60 characters naming no data (G-T2.4); one notice, many pointers (G-T2.5); T2 named in every state row (G-T2.6). The legs run in the server's order: view-as, then `recording:write`, then the row's state (`D02`:641; `D03`:143; `D04`:168; `D05`:107). **Checks** reads "1a–d · 2 · 3 · 4 · 6" when every item holds (and "· 5" for a panel notice); every disabled-looking control is classed (R918 BQ-07's check) with the parents' own class words, and **no exit is gated** (D04 A3; D05 A3). 17 <!-- audit:t2=17 --> rows.

## 9. R1–R7 per surface (RULED BQ-11; `DS`:65–:83)

Each cell is a cite or a row. **R5** is **n/a for every recorder write**: no arm token touches these routes; the purge and retention paths are step-up flows (`22`:15425; `22`:5396), never arm gates; the C-2.6 carve-out does not reach these surfaces (`D03`:143). **R6**: nothing is pinned unmerged — D03-G1…G5 (`D03`:415–:419), D04-G1…G12 (`D04`:462–:473) and D05-G1 (`D05`:401) are PROPOSED and not served at the base (no preview route, no `next_sweep_at`, no per-tier growth, no `backfilled` on `gaps[]`); the purge route as built (`22`:15414–:15461) is the pinned truth; re-read before every commit. **R7**: page name + id only, by cite (`D02`:794; `D03`:65; `D04`:5; `D05`:5); no new page (RULED BQ-17); the tool's host / URL / identifier scan (G15-HYGIENE).

## 10. Clause verdicts, the WCAG 2.2 cross-walk and the colour audits (RULED BQ-12)

Verdicts per R-3. An `at risk` row names its Major F row or its interim path in the verdict cell (the consuming story's build rule, E29-D05 R-3's form); a `non-compliant` row carries a Blocking F row; a row any Minor-or-above F row cites (by its `05` clause, its `05` line or its WCAG number in the F row's closing tag) is never `compliant` and its Row cell names those rows; the tool checks both tables (G9-VERDICT).

### 10.3 Contrast: the union of the parents' pair lists, re-measured

The tool re-measures the union of E16-D02 §10.4's rows (parsed at the base, `D02`:594–:613) and the E16-D03 / D04 / D05 audits' `PAIRS_TABLE`s (imported) in dark, light and high contrast through the E16-D05 audit's resolver and `contrast_check.py`'s formula. Bars: 4.5:1 text, 3:1 non-text (`05`:125–:126), gated; the high-contrast theme's 7:1 AAA-leaning bar (`05`:130) reported, not gated, as the parents report it; a pair whose side is not a token is reported, not gated; `color.text.disabled` is never drawn (`D02`:629; `D03`:527; `D04`:605; `D05`:487). The unregistered pairs (`D02H`:40–:46; `D03`:527 7 PROPOSED; `D04`:605 13 PROPOSED; `D05`:487 10 PROPOSED) are **G-03**, one G row to the `tools/contrast` owner with E16-S01.

**Union:** 52 token pairs · gated FAIL 0 at 4.5 / 3 · gated minima text 4.76, non-text 3.17, hc-text 4.54 · under the 7:1 AAA-leaning bar in high contrast: 2 (`color.text.link` on `color.surface.raised` (6.66); `color.text.on-action` on `color.status.danger.default` (4.54)), reported · 0 pair reported, not gated (a non-token side) <!-- audit:contrast -->

The 7:1 bar has no hard gate — RULED (orchestrator, 2026-10-30, #374 comment 6519414520): the tool reports it and never gates on it; the two pairs stay F-18, Minor, to the DS owner by E05's token route, carried with G-03's unregistered pairs.

### 10.4 Colour never the only signal: the set's carrier families

Each carrier is a word, a glyph, a shape or a geometry; colour reinforces. For the measurable pairs the tool reports the greyscale distance ΔL\* (CIE L\* of the Rec. 601 grey) and the worst ΔE76 under protanopia, deuteranopia and tritanopia, each the minimum over the three themes. A small number is not a defect: it says the carrier, not the colour, does the work. The committed CVD renders are cited, nothing redrawn: D02 boards 01 and 06 (`D02`:811–:812), D03 boards 03 and 06 (`D03`:95–:96), D05 boards 02 and 05 (`D05`:69); **SCR-142 has none** and `D04` §15 computes no CVD distance (`D04`:605) — the tool computes CR-02 and CR-03 for it here, and a committed render is **F-15**, routed to E16-D07 (#565). 8 <!-- audit:cr=8 --> families.

| ----- | ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------- | -------------- | -------------------------- |

Two families collapse somewhere and stay distinct by their carriers: the disk level's primary fill against the warning fill in greyscale (CR-02 — the number, the unit and the state word carry) and the state icons' tones (CR-01 — the shapes carry). The numbers are reported, not gated, as the parents report them.

**Motion (`T Tech`).** `D02` §9.2 is compliant: the disk bar and the purge / compact bar tween over `motion.duration.normal` and **jump** under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`, the starting spinner pulses, everything else is never animated (`D02`:534–:539); `D04`:381 names the meter's pair. **SCR-141's job-footer progress bar** (`D03`:371 names the duration token but no reduce twin for the footer bar as `D02`:537 does) and **SCR-142's jobs-strip and import bars** (`D04`:376 names `progress` with no pair; `D04`:381's pair is the meter's) name no `motion-reduce` pair → **F-12**, **F-13** (Minor); on SCR-047 / SCR-151 nothing animates (`D05`:269). **Tabular figures (`T Perf`).** `D02`:526 (`type.num.sm`, tabular), `D03`:362 (`type.num.base`), `D05`:328 (`type.num.sm` for the mono timestamp); **`D04`** binds `type.num.sm` to the meters' value and `type.num.base` to the card rows (`D04`:373–:374) but names no numeric token for the per-symbol table's cells or the strip's percentage → **F-14** (Minor). **Targets.** 32 × 32 comfortable and 24 × 24 compact (`D02H`:116–:119), above `05`:100's 24; D-17's 44 px ask (`D02`:779) is **F-16** (Info).

### 12.5 Heuristic findings (HR rows; no participant was seen)

- **HR-1** `(heuristic)` A screen-reader user told "Stop is unavailable while a position is open." at the point of action learns the rule faster than one told "unavailable"; the five `recorder.*` sentences are the right length for a live region, under 60 characters, so the shell announcer never queues a paragraph behind the summary batch.
- **HR-2** `(heuristic)` Cancel-first on every typed dialog (C-a) costs one `Tab` before the typed field and buys one habit across the set: a user who has learned the purge dialog on SCR-140 meets the same first stop on SCR-141 and SCR-142.
- **HR-3** `(heuristic)` "Nothing is missing or broken: CandleViewer keeps only what it records." read once on entry does what the stem alone cannot: it closes the "is this an error?" question before the user reaches the CTA; the gap family's cause sentences do the opposite work and should never share the sentence (BQ-16 RULED in D05).

3 <!-- audit:hr=3 --> heuristics.

## 13. The screen-reader script for E16-Q05 (derived from the bound attributes; nothing run here; RULED BQ-08, BQ-14, BQ-21)

These are expected-rendering scripts derived from the bound names, roles, states, descriptions and the live-region rows of §6, in `05` §10's template shape (`05`:257–:261), one block per reviewed surface. **Nothing here claims a screen-reader run.** They are **part of E16-S01 / S03 / S04 / S05's Definition of Done** (`02`:76), binding, not optional; E16-Q05 (#482) is the a11y executor and reuses the Features verbatim; E16-Q01 (#404) takes them as its black-box charter: a built utterance that differs is a defect in one of the two and is reconciled there. The assistive technology and its version are E16-Q05's to pick from `05`'s coverage matrix (`05`:217–:222); every AT-dependent clause is `not yet decidable — routed to E16-Q05 (#482)`. Strings carry the parents' fixture words only (R-6). 5 <!-- audit:gherkin-features=5 --> features, 18 <!-- audit:gherkin-scenarios=18 --> scenarios.

```gherkin
Feature: Recorder admin by keyboard and screen reader — accessibility acceptance criteria (SCR-140, CMP-177)

  Scenario: Reading the page (KB-01, KB-02; RG-02, RG-03; SR-01)
    Given the Recorder page is open with four symbols recording
    When the user tabs to the grid "Recorded symbols"
    Then the current row is read as "BTCUSDT, manual, recording since 1 Sep 2026, 34.8 GB, keep forever on"
    And the 1 Hz value changes never move focus and are never announced
    And a state change is spoken once in the next 10 s batch, "ETHUSDT degraded; SOLUSDT stopped recording"

  Scenario: A blocked Stop (T2-01; SR-14; FM-18)
    Given focus is on SOLUSDT's Stop, whose chart is open
    Then the button is "Stop recording SOLUSDT", aria-disabled, with "Not available" leading it and the reason as its description
    When the user presses Enter
    Then nothing is sent and "Recording stops 30 min after the last chart closes." is spoken once, politely, by the shell announcer
    And the summary region stays silent

  Scenario: The Why? popover (KB-04; FM-05, FM-06)
    Given focus is on "Why is SOLUSDT recorded?"
    When the user presses Enter
    Then the popover opens with focus on "Keep recording" and is read by role
    When the user presses Esc, or presses Keep recording
    Then focus returns to "Why is SOLUSDT recorded?" and the summary batch names the row's new state (F-03)

  Scenario: The purge dialog (KB-07; SD-01, SD-02, SD-03; SR-09, SR-12)
    Given the user presses Enter on "Purge data…"
    Then the dialog is read by role with its title "Purge ADAUSDT data?" and focus is on Cancel
    And the body is read, "It cannot be undone, and replay, footprint history and the journal lose this range."
    When the user tabs to the typed field
    Then its label is "Type the symbol to confirm" and the field is empty
    When the purge runs
    Then "Purge data…" is busy and the purge line is spoken once, politely; its percentage is never spoken per tick
    And "Purged 5.5 GB of ADAUSDT data" is spoken once, politely, at the end (F-08)
```

```gherkin
Feature: Retention policy by keyboard and screen reader — accessibility acceptance criteria (SCR-141)

  Scenario: Opening the editor (KB-09; RG-12)
    Given the user presses Enter on "Retention policy" in the disk card

  Scenario: The sweep's job states (SR-21, SR-22, SR-23; BG-02; F-09)
    Given Apply now was ticked
    Then "Retention run queued" is spoken once at the start and the running percentage is never spoken per poll
    And "Retention run succeeded" or "Retention run cancelled" is spoken once, politely, or "Retention run failed ({code})" once, assertively

  Scenario: Invalid fields (SR-17, SR-18; F-10)
```

## 14. The keyboard-only five-task walkthrough (written substitute; RULED BQ-21)

`T Test`'s "keyboard-only prototype run" and `T DoD`'s walkthrough are written here as a numbered key sequence from the merged tables (`05`:230's form) and as §13's derived tables; **no session was run and none is reported** (`T Adapt`). The run is E16-Q05's (#482) and the owner's. Every step names its key, where focus lands and what is spoken; the tool fails a step without a key (G25-XREF reads the ids). 11 <!-- audit:ks=11 --> steps.

### 15.2 The source ladder (one rung per column; each departure a C row)

1. **Server data and behaviour:** `22` / `23`, then `04` where it constrains (SR-099, the RBAC rows); never a design spec's invented field (§16.3 carries the gaps by id).
2. **Security-bearing properties:** `04` and `22`'s own text over any drawn state or string (§20.3), worded neutrally.
3. **Visible state, keyboard, focus and announcements:** this review's rows over D02–D05 over `14`'s sketch — each departure an F row (§11) or a C row (§12.1).
4. **Strings:** the parents' decks verbatim (`D02` §8, `D03` §14, `D04` §14, `D05` §14), `new` only as PROPOSED (the deck's `status`).
5. **Component ids and contracts:** `15` at the base; CMP-177's contract as D02 §4.2 shipped it (`15`:2188).
6. **Token names:** `packages/ui/tokens/*.tokens.json` through `themes.json`; `color.surface.admin-tint` MISSING (D02 D-12, D04-C4); `pattern.hatch.*` registry ids (E11-D05).

### 16.4 Declared problem codes per route (every code `22` declares on a §16.1 operation, routed; the tool reads them from `22` — G26)

A refusal renders a deck sentence, runs the step-up flow, or renders the generic **T4** form — the CMP-027 sentence with the code token in parentheses, inline under the control, polite once, the control kept enabled (`D04`:182; `D04`:315's form; T2-11) — and the problem body's `message` / `detail` (`22`:17434) is **never rendered**. Where a code has no named sentence, the sentence is a G row to its parent's next text touch and the consuming story is the lane (BQ-22); the validity look (T2-10) pre-empts the codes a client can decide before the request. 19 <!-- audit:codes-routed=19 --> rows route 18 <!-- audit:codes-declared=18 --> declared codes.

`403 session_read_only` after three invalid step-up codes (`22`:12833–:12834) is the shell's state (E42-D02), not a recorder refusal: every write renders T4 until it lifts.

## 17. Redlines, tokens and breakpoints (RULED BQ-13; consolidated, **tool-lifted, never measured**)

Every cell below equals its source cell in the parent's token map (`D02` §11.1, `D03` §11, `D04` §11, `D05` §11), parsed by the tool (G18-REDLINES); a cell the parents do not carry stays theirs. No value is read off a PNG. **Breakpoints** as the parents state them: 1280 × 800 and 200 % (`D02`:121; `D04`:194; `D05`:320); SCR-141 960 px wide, max-height 85 vh (`D03`:59); the admin nav 240 px assumed (`D02`:770); comfortable density, 32 px rows (`D02`:57; `D03`:172; `D04`:194); compact 24 px (`D02H`:119). 35 <!-- audit:redline-rows=35 --> rows lifted.

## 20. The QA artefact: the deck, the per-screen checklist and the frozen set (RULED BQ-14)

### 20.1 The deck (`E16-D06-copy.json`)

The deck holds 281 <!-- audit:deck=281 --> string entries — one per row of `D02` §8, `D03` §14, `D04` §14 and `D05` §14 283 <!-- audit:parent-deck-rows=283 --> parent rows; by parent 24/89/102/66 <!-- audit:deck-by-parent=24/89/102/66 -->; `rbac.view_as` is one entry with three sources) — keyed by the parents' own keys (`recorder.*`, `retention.*`, `storage.*`, `empty.*`, `chip.*`, `gap.*`, `rbac.*`, `notice.*`), else a stable row id derived from the cite (`d02.8.rNN`). Entry fields: `parent · row_id · surface · state · string (the quoted strings) · sources (each parent's cell verbatim) · cite · provenance · status · frozen`. 182 <!-- audit:deck-proposed=182 --> entries are PROPOSED (the parents' `new` rows and D02's five `recorder.*` keys); 22 <!-- audit:deck-frozen=22 --> carry a §20.3 row. The deck's `script` mirrors §6.1's 35 <!-- audit:deck-script=35 --> SR rows (the a11y script E16-Q05 and E16-Q01 reuse). The tool parses the parents' decks at the base and fails on any row missing or any cell not verbatim (G16-DECK: 0 missing).

### 20.3 The frozen security-bearing set (each row a text or behaviour · property · what breaks if reworded · hook; read by the Fable light lens before the ready line — RULED BQ-10, BQ-14, BQ-19)

**Every row is quoted from merged public text and names no review record**; security-bearing rules are worded neutrally; a row is reworded only with design and security review. 9 <!-- audit:frozen=9 --> rows.

| #      | Text or behaviour                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Property                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Breaks if reworded `(heuristic)`                                                                                                       | Hook (deck key / test)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| (i)    | "RBAC denies server-side; the UI presents." (`14`:85): a `/admin/*` destination is absent from navigation and hard-404s for a session without the admin grant; every gate here is presentation of the server's answer                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | the UI renders what the server allows (C-12.4)                                                                                                                                                                                           | a client-side check presented as the control invites a build that trusts it                                                            | test: every write of §8 refused by the server renders T4 at the point of action, never a client decision                                                                                                                                                                  |
| (ii)   | the purge is "Irreversibly delete recorded data (owner, step-up `recorder_purge`)" (`22`:15418) — `recording:write` (`22`:15461), the `recorder_purge` step-up, no-grace (`22`:15425), a reason, the required `before`, and the typed symbol drawn with "**the typed field empty — never pre-filled**" (`D04`:320)                                                                                                                                     | one destructive route, one shape, the typed echo never pre-filled                                                                                                                                                                        | a pre-filled or auto-filled typed field removes the confirmation the route is built around                                             | `storage.purge.typed`, `storage.purge.before`, `storage.purge.confirm`; test: the typed field mounts empty                                                                                                                                                                |
| (iii)  | a pin or a retention change: "`pinned` or `retention_days` in the body needs the `recorder`" step-up (`22`:5396), run on submit after the typed confirmation (`D02`:383)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | step-up is a flow on submit, never a gate drawn as disabled                                                                                                                                                                              | a disabled control in place of the flow hides the path; a skipped step-up ships an unprotected write                                   | `retention.stepup.code`, `storage.purge.stepup`; test: the request is held and re-sent once with the same key                                                                                                                                                             |
| (iv)   | "Retention jobs MUST honour pins, run dry-run-first with a report, and audit every deletion (symbol, range, byte count)." (`04`:958): Apply keeps the validity look until the preview has rendered for the current diff (`D03`:392)                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | the server's retention job runs dry-run-first and reports (`04`:958); the UI presents that order — Apply after the preview — and, until D03-G1 is served, the preview is a client-side estimate, not the job's report (`D03`:392; §16.3) | an Apply enabled before the preview hides the order the job enforces; a preview captioned as the report misstates what the session saw | `retention.apply.preview_first`, `retention.preview.title`; test: Apply is validity-disabled until the preview renders; server: SR-099's integration test (`04`:958) is the control's test, the backend's, not E16-S03's                                                  |

(`D02`:506)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | the consequence is text in the dialog, read by role                                                                                                                                                                                      | a shorter body drops the window, the size or the irreversibility                                                                       | `storage.purge.body`, `retention.confirm.body`, `retention.confirm.body_now`; test: each body carries the three parts                                                                                                                                                     |
| (ix)   | SCR-142 draws "no data-store console link or port" (`D04`:164)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | the admin surface links no store console                                                                                                                                                                                                 | a console link exposes a store the UI never addresses                                                                                  | test: no anchor on SCR-142 targets a store host or port                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

**Never rendered or written:** a server problem body's `message` / `detail` raw — every code a recorder route declares is mapped to a deck sentence by its parent or routed in §16.4 (the step-up flow or the generic T4 form with the code token), never rendered from the body; a symbol, count or size in a `recorder.*` / `rbac.*` reason; "Not recording" on a restricted frame; a pre-filled typed symbol; any string outside the deck.

Nothing binary is added by this PR.

## 22. The engineering walkthrough: written, not held (RULED BQ-16; G-05)

**No session was held and none is claimed.** `T Scope` bullet 4's walkthrough and `T DoD`'s "held" read as four written parts: (a) the ≤ 10-item owner walkthrough posted on #374 at the ready line (A28; the parents' own lists `D02`:714–:725, `D03`:589, `D04`:668 and `D05`:548 cited, not repeated); (b) the story-role dry run — one fresh-context read per consumer (E16-S01 #484, S03 #485, S04 #486, S05 #487, S02 #566, Q01 #404, Q05 #482), given only this file, the deck and the ticket's text, listing the values it needs with the pointer found or "not found" (the synonym search before any "not found"), plus the delegated FE-lead read (the component skeleton of SCR-140, SCR-141, SCR-142 and the SCR-047 block from the pack alone: ids, states, strings, keys, data sources); every "not found" folded before merge or carried as a G / Q row with an owner — **never a new issue** (`T AC3`'s "follow-up design ticket" is C-h); (c) the Q&A, permanent here and posted as one comment on #374; (d) each consumer's acknowledgement = its build-plan restatement on its own issue at claim time.

### 22.1 The owner walkthrough protocol (≤ 10 items; posted on #374 at the ready line; A28)

1. On SCR-140's `stop-refused` board (17), press SOLUSDT's Stop and read the reason: is one sentence enough to act on? (T2-01, SR-14)
2. Open the Why? popover, press Keep recording: say where focus should land (F-03, the PROPOSED default).
3. On the purge dialog (board 19), read the body cold and say what, how much and whether it can be undone; confirm the first stop is Cancel (C-a).
4. Step SCR-140's flow across its thirteen undrawn boards as text, D02's own checks ("a step through a board not drawn is text", `D02`:714; board 14's Health line, `D02`:719): for `starting`, `purge-in-progress`, `empty`, `loading`, `unavailable`, `stale`, `purge-failed`, `disk-card`, `row-detail`, `add-symbol`, `stop-confirm`, `retention-confirm` and `motion`, say whether §18's row and the deck's strings are enough to build the state without a drawing (`D02`:794, RULED).
5. On SCR-141's confirm board (03), say why the first stop moves from the typed input to Cancel (F-01) and whether one more `Tab` is acceptable; on its job board (06), say when the footer should speak: at the start and the end only (F-09).
6. On SCR-142's states board (03), read "Recording pauses before trading is affected." and say what else the banner must say (SD-06); on its import board (07), say whether a skipped file should ever be announced (SR-25: never).
7. On SCR-047's roles board (03), read the sentence form and say whether "Only the owner can change recording." is the right verb (D05-C8, cited).
8. On the gap-band board (05), move the data cursor through the gap and say what you expect to hear inside and on the first slot out (SR-33).
9. Read §13's derived spoken forms for the three clauses that stay `not yet decidable — routed to E16-Q05 (#482)` — §10.1's §8 protocol row and §10.2's 1.4.10 and "4.1.3 as a screen reader renders it" (F-07, F-08, F-10) — and say which rendering you expect; they are decided by E16-Q05's pass, not here.
10. Decide the five PROPOSED defaults of §1.6 (or confirm them) and the two 7:1 pairs of F-18.

### 22.2 The story-role dry run and the FE-lead read (G-WALK)

Filled 2026-10-30 from eight written reads (one per consumer and the FE lead), each given only this file, the deck and its ticket's text at the pre-fold head; the counts are **self-counted by an independent fresh-context reader** (a "Found" is the reader's own call; a partial answer counts as the reader classed it) and never a consumer's acknowledgement. Every "not found" has one disposition: a **pointer** (the answer stands in a parent or another merged document — §22.4, by consumer), a **C** row (the ticket's text disagrees with the merged, Done design: the design wins and the ticket is corrected by a comment at its claim, never here), a **G** row or a **Q** row (a true gap: one owner, due the consumer's sprint start). 8 <!-- audit:walk=8 --> reads; 24 <!-- audit:walk-qa=24 --> questions answered in §22.3; 72 <!-- audit:walk-pointers=72 --> pointer rows in §22.4.

### 22.3 The walkthrough protocol's questions and answers

The readers' questions, deduplicated, each answered by the row that holds the answer (posted as one comment on #374; BQ-16 (c)). The standing answers the review already gives: _Where is the keyboard model?_ §3, by id, never re-typed. _Where does focus go after each operation?_ §4; the four destinations the parents left to a rule are F-03…F-06 with their PROPOSED defaults. _Which strings are announced, where and how often?_ §6 and §7; the deck's `script` mirrors them. _Which controls are T2, and what does a press do?_ §8; a press re-shows the reason once, politely, through the shell announcer. _What is gated server-side?_ §20.3 rows (i)–(v). _Which fields does each element bind to?_ §16. _Which stories are required?_ §19, never "built". _What may not be reworded?_ §20.3.

1. **Which permission gates the admin route, and what does a session without `recording:write` see — absent controls or T2 locks?** (#484, #486, #487, #404, #566) — C-r: the route is the admin grant (T2-05), the data reads `recording:read`, a write without the grant is T2 `aria-disabled` + lock by the session's permissions (T2-03, T2-04); a Manager holding the contract's grant sees the CTA until G-04 / Q-02 lands; §20.3 rows (i) and (v).
2. **Is pin pessimistic through step-up, which piece asks for the code, and where does unpin live?** (#484, #485) — C-s: the `recorder` step-up on submit (§20.3 row (iii)); the step-up modal is SCR-006 (§22.4 S01); unpin is SCR-140's Keep-forever-off dialog and SCR-141 links to it.
3. **Where are SCR-140's column table, the Depth / Streams options, the stop-confirm body, the Why? body, the add dialog and the toast texts?** (#484) — §22.4 (S01), every one in D02 by line; C-t: there are no toasts.
4. **Which calls are Stop and Record again, are the status / storage GETs the polling pair, and what is the poll interval?** (#484) — §16.1: `removeRecordedSymbol` and `addRecordedSymbol`; the three GETs paint the first state; Q-04: no REST poll is designed.
5. **Do the pack's disk and purge strings replace the ticket's, and what is the visual baseline for the thirteen text-bound SCR-140 states?** (#484) — C-t; §18 (text-bound, RULED) with E16-D07 (#565)'s design QA of the built surfaces as the review (§24.1).
6. **Inversion: a client-side check with submit disabled, or the post-422 summary?** (#485) — C-u; §16.4 row 8 (`retention.refused.invalid`).
7. **Are "leased" and "inside hot window" protected reasons needed?** (#485) — C-u: only "Exempt (pinned)" is drawn; the other holds stay server-side.
8. **Is a destructive PUT retried on a network error, what triggers "Not sent", and where are the debounce and the ETag conflict state?** (#485) — Q-05 (nothing re-sent but the one step-up re-send); C-v (a Preview button, no ETag state).
9. **Do the tickets' analytics names and perf budgets stand?** (#485, #486, #487) — C-z: the parents' names where a parent gives one, the story's own key otherwise; Q-06: budgets are the stories' `T Perf`, not design.
10. **Which maintenance set does SCR-142 build — tier migration, toasts, purge?** (#486) — C-w: compact, vacuum (PROPOSED), the reused purge dialog and import; no toast; §22.4 (S04).
11. **What series feeds each sparkline, what do the tier tables show for oldest / newest, and what is the under-24-samples variant?** (#486) — §16.3 D04-G1 / D04-G2 (unserved; the captioned PROPOSED insets meanwhile, §22.4 S04); C-w: no "collecting data" variant is designed.
12. **Stale at 5 minutes or another threshold, and is there a manual Refresh?** (#486) — §22.4 (S04): stale after 2 × 60 s from `generated_at`, the stamp's Retry when stale; C-w.
13. **What does the strip say while queued, what does Stop job confirm, how does a reload find the job, what are the import labels and path, does the summary add rows written?** (#486) — G-08 (the queued line); §16.3 D04-G10 (Stop job PROPOSED); C-w (no resume by id, the shipped summary line); §22.4 (S04) for the labels and the path.
14. **What role, landmark and announcement does the migration-pending banner carry?** (#486) — G-08.
15. **What are SCR-047's reasons 1a–6 and their inputs, headings, actions and precedence?** (#487) — §22.4 (S05): the reasons are 1a / 1b / 2 / 3 at `E11-D04`:338–:343 with D05's fact-to-state table; C-x: "1a–d · 2 · 3 · 4 · 6" in §8.1 is the G-T2 check list, not a reason set.
16. **What are the strings, component and timeout for "coverage request failed" with Retry, and what shows before it?** (#487) — G-09 (the words); the component is CMP-026 `variant="error"` (`15`:310); loading is CMP-029 under `aria-busy`, bounded by the bootstrap timeout (`D05`:125).
17. **Is the keyboard-reachable gap list binding, or the cursor and the description?** (#487, #482) — C-x: the band is never a stop; the chart region's description, the cursor readout and the panel's hidden gap sentence carry the gaps.
18. **UTC strings or the user's configured timezone?** (#487) — C-x: UTC text everywhere (`E11-D04`:345).
19. **Which document holds the auto-start notice, is the SCR-041 / SCR-100 element a dot or the chip text, and is Stop one click or a confirm?** (#566) — C-y: no notice (decision 2), the chip in text; G-06 for the undrawn compact forms; §22.4 (S02): Stop opens the §6.2 confirm, a blocked Stop is T2.
20. **What are the unknown-state words and the watchlist "no symbol recorded" copy?** (#566) — G-06.
21. **Which tokens, stories and keyboard rows cover the chip menu, and is a state change while mounted announced?** (#566) — §22.4 (S02): CMP-041's keys (`15`:484), the hosts' own live regions (`14`:2263); G-06 for the tokens of the undrawn forms.
22. **Which role behaviour, oracles, step-up failures, open-position semantics and fixtures does the black-box plan assert?** (#404) — Q-07; C-r; §22.4 (Q01); §16.3's Meanwhile column for every PROPOSED field.
23. **What is the 60 s utterance fail line, which axe states and Lighthouse bar, what to expect for the started / purge-started strings, the picker's no-trap exit, the hatch's pixel bar, 400 % and 1.4.12?** (#482) — Q-08; Q-01 / G-01 / G-02 (the strings: expected absent, not a fail); §22.4 (Q05): CMP-048's `Esc` closes the listbox and keeps the dialog (`15`:566).
24. **Which flags, merge order and shared-component owners, is the contract merged and generated, where is the row-state derivation, how is the deck consumed, who owns R-340?** (FE lead) — Q-06; G-07 (the derivation); §22.4 (FE): the amendment fields and `RecorderUpdate` are merged and generated as `Recorder` in `packages/protocol/src/generated/ws/index.ts`; the web ws client has no `recorder` topic yet — the consuming story adds it.

### 24.5 Versioning

v1 at the base named in the tool's pins. A late change is any merged change to a pinned input; it lands as a new version row posted once on each affected consumer with "re-sync needed: <what changed>" (`16` §13.5; L1-80, L1-102). The pins are re-written only by `--repin-ruled`, a deliberate act read in the diff; `--check` never re-pins.
